Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the generation framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 36. The resulting operator achieves bound O(802/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_36(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 36. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^802(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The generation operator L associated with invariance principle theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 902. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 335 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
